# THE OBSERVANCE - IMPROVEMENT AUDIT (red-team + prioritization)

> **2026-07-07 status note:** this is a historical July 1 red-team snapshot, not the current launch
> verdict. Several findings below have since been fixed or superseded. Use `design/CURRENT-READINESS-VERDICT.md`
> and `design/RUNBOOK.md` for current go/no-go status; keep this file as provenance for why the fixes
> were made.
>
> A rigorous, grounded "why could this break / where is it not as good as it could be / how to
> improve" pass across the whole project, written **2026-07-01** against HEAD. Every finding cites
> real files/lines. writes 400 on the live schema, and `schema-repair.sql` does NOT fix
   two of them.** `event_log` inserts (every subsystem's audit/error log), `bases` upserts (base
   detection), and `settings` reads (remote watcher-sleep) all mismatch the real columns/types. The
   "it must run" guardrail is failing in ways the docs claim are resolved. (`EventLogRow.java`,
   `BaseDetector.java:107`, `SettingsRow.java:16`.)

2. **[RESOLVED] Rich per-keeper world templates and the rune-ring rosetta are wired into placement.**
   `placeroom`, `placeregion`, `placedeep`, and `placeworld` now call `StructureTemplates.keeper(...)`,
   and `tools/check_world_build_readiness.ps1` fails if those paths regress to the generic fallback.

3. **[RESOLVED] Five signature beats are registered and guarded.** `RevealBeat`,
   `RoomSwapBeat`, `KeeperNpcBeat`, `ModeledMobBeat`, and `SpatialVoiceBeat` are present in
   `BeatLibrary.registerDefaults()`, and `tools/check_plugin_compile.ps1` fails if that wiring drifts.

4. **[RESOLVED] Resource-pack version metadata and audio are built and guarded.** `pack.mcmeta` now
   uses the pinned 1.21.11 version range, the pack ships 11 OGG files, and the asset/media checks fail
   if format metadata, sound references, zip freshness, or rune-font geometry drift.

5. **[RESOLVED 2026-07-07] Companion/reckoning/co-op producers are wired and guarded.**
   `WrenNpcListener`, `CompanionArcWatcher`, `CoopPlateListener`, and `SeventhChoiceListener` now produce the
   Wren trust/reveal/reckoning flags, co-op world-leg marker, and Seventh restore/erase choice. Discord consumes
   the companion event log/flags and `#the-record` co-op word leg. `tools/check_companion_arc_contracts.ps1` is
   wired into `tools/audit_all.ps1`; remaining risk is live rehearsal of Wren spawn, reckoning markers, co-op
   plate/carve+word timing, and Seventh choice markers on the real server.
6. **[P1] The "salience showrunner" is a deterministic clue-drip, not salience.** `decide.ts` orders
   the next thread by `outcome_rank 7. **[P1] The record website is an un-wired read-only shell, and its one live view was never
   migrated.** `record/[slug]/page.tsx:95` reads `v_record`, which **no migration creates**, so the
   archive is permanently frozen at baseline. The headline "write answers INTO the record" feature
   does not exist. 8. **[P1] The world-build promise is still the biggest player-facing risk.** Earlier claims that the
   Undercroft datapack was an empty void are resolved/deferred, but launch still depends on replacing
   authoring placeholder coordinates with real placed sites and proving the overworld route in-client.
   The world is the #1 driver of "amazing" and remains the surface most dependent on manual placement proof.

9. **[P1] Per-player illusion is missing its core primitives.**
   `PerPlayer` has `fakeBlock`/`sound`/`particle`/`title` but **no `showEntity`, no packet light, no
   per-player fog**. The asymmetric co-op vault and the name-on-the-wall scare both depend on these
   and cannot be built until they land. (`util/PerPlayer.java`.)

10. **[RESOLVED] The back half used to be one wrong seed-apply from either dark or leaking.**
    `npm run db:seed` now regenerates `discord/supabase/apply-all.sql`, which owns both database
    lineages, places 0006/0007/0008 before every seed, includes the later launch migrations, and ends
    with schema repair. `npm run audit` runs `db:bundlecheck`, so drift fails loudly.

---

The engine's spine is in better
  shape than its own overview claims. Trust the code.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Five signature beats are now registered.** The compile check guards
  `RevealBeat`, `RoomSwapBeat`, `KeeperNpcBeat`, `ModeledMobBeat`, and `SpatialVoiceBeat` in
  `BeatLibrary.registerDefaults()`. Historical pre-fix note: before this wiring, these authored
  beat payloads could sit in `beat_queue` without a matching handler.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Beat double-fire on a fast restart / second instance.** `BeatQueuePoller` now
  atomically claims `approved -> firing` in Supabase before enactment; the DB CHECK includes `firing`.
  Historical pre-fix note: `BeatQueuePoller` claims beats
  only with an **in-JVM** `inFlight` set (`BeatQueuePoller.java:42,84`); the durable guard is the
  post-enact async `markBeatDecided` PATCH. Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â a real but thin subset.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] The rune font is now used on in-world surfaces.** `StructureTemplates` rune crib
  signs and rune-bearing beats set `observance:runes`, while `tools/check_minecraft_text_surfaces.ps1`,
  `tools/check_assets.ps1`, and dashboard/pack rune selftests guard fit, atlas geometry, and cross-surface
  alphabet drift. Remaining caveat: because the bitmap maps ASCII A-Z/0-9, any mixed plain/rune text still
  needs deliberate component separation rather than a future PUA remap.

### Concrete improvements
- Keep the registration, DB-claim, pack/rune, and world-template guards inside the full audit so these fixed
  July 1 blockers do not silently return.
- Gate ignition on a **specific block** (the sign/lectern face) or require sneak-right-click, so
  incidental interaction cannot ignite.
- Build `PerPlayer.showEntity` + packet light + per-player fog before Phase 3.

Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â THE DB + SCHEMA

Two Supabase lineages that **do not share history**: `dashboard/supabase/migrations` is the canonical
base and public view lineage; `discord/supabase/migrations` extends it with the oracle, threads,
observations, rate limits, and showrunner lock. The launch path is now the generated
`discord/supabase/apply-all.sql` bundle, not loose per-folder application. It includes both lineages
in dependency order and is checked by `npm run audit`.

### What could break (confirmed 400s the plugin will throw against the live schema)
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] `event_log` inserts now match the live schema.** `EventLogRow` serializes only `level`, `source`, `message`, and `created_at`; the plugin DB contract check guards this.
  message, mc_uuid, detail, created_at`; the table has only `level, source, message, created_at`
  (`dashboard/0001_init.sql:115-121`). PGRST204 "column not found." The dashboard + bot write the
  correct `{level,source,message}` `isWatcherSleeping()` fails-open to `false` (`SupabaseClient.java:229`). The dashboard's
  "watcher sleep" switch **cannot mute the plugin**; only the local config kill-switch works.

### Not as good as it could be
- **[P1-D5] dossier/compliance/base drift is repaired for WRITES but orphaned for READS.**
  schema-repair adds the plugin's flat columns so writes stop 400ing, but the dashboard still reads
  the *original* columns (`Dossiers.tsx:76-105` reads `solo_ratio/blocks_mined/group_distance/

- **Flag flow is atomic and race-safe.** `observance_merge_arc_flags` does `flags = flags || p_flags`
  in one statement (`0006:57-68`), `security definer`, service-role-only. `arc_state` is single-row
  (`id=1` check). Both surfaces AND-test `requires_flags` in app code with a byte-parity gate

Java `FlagGate`). `insertSolveIfNew` + `solves unique(puzzle_key,player_id)`
  makes concurrent solves single-reward. No lost-update on flags.

  `revoke all from anon/authenticated`; only three SECURITY-DEFINER views are anon-readable and they
  expose no names/labels. `0003_lockdown.sql` closed a real hole (0001 had granted `authenticated for
  all using(true)` on all base tables). **Confirm 0003 is applied to Braden's project before go-live - Seeds are idempotent (`on conflict` upserts); `apply-all.sql` ordering runs the flag/kind/quorum
  migrations before the guarded activation UPDATEs, so the historical "guards no-op" bug is resolved.

Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â DATAPACK + RESOURCE PACK

### What could break
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Resource-pack format metadata matches the pinned server.** `resourcepack/pack.mcmeta`
  now uses `min_format`/`max_format` `[75,0]` for MC 1.21.11, and `tools/check_assets.ps1` guards both
  resourcepack `[75,0]` and datapack `[94,1]` metadata.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Audio is no longer absent.** `sounds.json` declares 11 events and every referenced
  OGG exists. `tools/check_media_readiness.ps1` verifies Vorbis/mono/duration, while `tools/check_assets.ps1`
  verifies the manifest-to-file references.
- **[RESOLVED/DEFERRED 2026-07-06] The Undercroft is not an accidental void, but it remains unfurnished.**
  The datapack now defines a noise-backed, solid deepslate dimension and `datapack/README.md` explicitly
  says not to teleport players there until a lit landing coordinate and authored gather-room/site exist.
- **[P2-R4] The reward advancements use `trigger: minecraft:impossible`** (`the_record_receives_you.
  json:19-21`) Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â correct pattern (they're plugin-granted via `AdvancementToastBeat`), but confirm the
  1.21.11 advancement `display.icon` schema (`{"id": "..."}`) still validates on 1.21.11; the item-
  component format tightened across 1.21.x. If it fails to parse, the datapack disables and toasts die.

### Not as good as it could be
- **[P2-R5] Undercroft biomes share the same Observance whisper motif.** This is no longer silence: the OGG
  exists and timing differs per biome. Differentiating cold/dark/void with unique ambience remains polish for
  the deferred Undercroft build, not a launch blocker while the dimension is unused.
- The rune font intentionally maps ASCII A-Z/0-9 while using the custom `observance:runes` font key;
  keep rune/plain text separated into distinct components so normal UI copy does not turn into glyphs.

### Concrete improvements
- Keep the asset checks wired into the full audit so resourcepack/datapack metadata, audio references, and
  rune font geometry cannot drift silently.
- When the Undercroft becomes player-facing, add a lit landing site/gather-room and then optionally split the
  shared whisper motif into distinct cold/dark/void ambience.
- Either build geometry inside the Undercroft (a floor + the gather-room) or keep it overworld-only and
  mark the dimension explicitly deferred so no one teleports into the void.

---

Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â STRUCTURES / VISUALS

### The concept + how the code-placed builds fall short
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Authored per-keeper templates are now placed.** `StructureTemplates` still owns
  the rich dispatcher (`rosetta`, `vaun`, `mara`, `sella`, `orin`, `brann`, `iss`, and the deep-half sites),
  and `ObservanceCommand` placement paths dispatch through `StructureTemplates.keeper(...)`. The world-build
  readiness check now guards `placeworld`, `placeroom`, `placeregion`, and `placedeep` against falling back
  to `keeperStone(...)` directly. Remaining launch risk is coordinate/build proof, not missing template wiring.
- **[P1-V2] The world is still a placed surface field, not a proven carved descent.** `placeregion` now uses
  non-uniform scatter and rich templates instead of a flat generic row, but launch readiness still depends on
  the operator placing/proving 42 real sites and judging whether the generated/placed field feels like a
  coherent descent in the real client. This is a manual rehearsal-quality risk, not a missing-code blocker.
- **[P1-V3] Reveal-safety is fine for beats but N/A for placement.** `StructureTemplates` deliberately
  places synchronously and unprotected (it's an admin build), so there's no "placed while witnessed"
  problem Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â but WORLD-BUILD's own note ("don't
  rely on the group guessing a sign is the input") stands: a first-timer may not realize *editing* the
  sign is how you answer. Consider a one-time in-fiction tell.

### Concrete improvements
- Keep `tools/check_world_build_readiness.ps1` in the full audit so placement paths continue using the rich
  `StructureTemplates.keeper(...)` dispatcher.
- During launch placement proof, judge whether the scattered field needs extra hand-built connective tissue
  to read as a descent rather than isolated surface set-pieces.
- Invest the limited builder-time in the R&D "cohesive procedural building" sub-task (tight palette,
  dark-default lighting, decay passes) OR accept a hand-built Minimum-Amazing region and drop the
  zero-manual promise `record_received` (`:533`). `prologue_ignited` is the single
  root. No dead gate on the critical path.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-07] P0-C1 companion/reckoning/co-op dead-gate finding is obsolete.**
  Producers and consumers now exist: Wren trust/reveal/reckoning, two in-world co-op legs, the Discord
  co-op word closer, and the Seventh restore/erase choice. The automated contract check guards this lane;
  the remaining work is live rehearsal evidence, not missing code.
- **[RESOLVED/OBSOLETE 2026-07-07] P1-C7 `companion-reveal` dangling-row warning is historical.**
  Current SQL only carries a defensive no-op update/comment for a future row, while the actual reveal is
  produced by `CompanionArcWatcher` via `companion_revealed` and consumed by the Wren showrunner lane.
- **[P2-C8] Literacy + token-laying are not actually enforced.** `rosetta_known` (set by the literacy
  on-ramp) and `tokens_laid` (set by `rite-tokens`) gate **no** puzzle via `requires_flags` the six
  keeper stones are ungated `active=true`, so a player can answer a keeper stone without ever solving
  the Rosetta, and the terminal rite's ordering leans on `next_puzzle_key` not a gate. If the design
  intends "must be literate first," it's un-enforced.

not reachability, collisions, hint coverage,
  or the ordering hazard. So C1/C3/C6/hint-gaps would all pass CI green. Add gate-reachability +
  collision + coverage checks to the self-test so these can't regress silently.

### Cross-answer collisions
- **[P1-C3] Live same-window collision: `the last marker is not the last`** on `stone-sella`
  (`puzzles_seed.sql:149`) **and** `seventh-shrine` (`:363`). Both can be OPEN at once (`stone-sella` is
  ungated `active=true`; `seventh-shrine` opens via Sella's next-clue). If a player types the phrase
  before solving Sella, the resolver resolves *Sella* (first unsolved in DB order) and the
  seventh-shrine payoff for that string never surfaces so after the 5 stones are dripped, the autonomy loop
  emits "pool empty" and there is **no in-world thread pointer for the rest of the game.** Findability
  effectively stops after Movement II. (`decide.ts:91`, `clue-specs.ts:111-299`.)
- **[P2-C5] No in-world "thread pointer."** With Discord's game-persona slated for retirement (Pillar
  4) and the record website un-wired (Area 6), nothing surfaces the next thread in-world. The soft-
  pressure "there is always one thread with momentum" promise has no in-world producer yet.

### Concrete improvements
- Keep `tools/check_companion_arc_contracts.ps1` in the audit suite and rehearse Wren spawn/trust,
  Iss-caught reveal, reckoning markers, co-op plate/carve+word, and Seventh choice markers end to end.
- Collapse the `the one who turned away` collision to one owner.
- Implement real salience + roster-awareness in `decide.ts`; add an in-world drip surface (the record
  block / a whisper) so world-primary findability doesn't depend on the retired Discord persona.
- Set-diff hints vs spine puzzles; author any missing tiers before real play.

- Service-role key is `import "server-only"` (`dashboard/src/lib/supabase/admin.ts:1`), never in a
  client bundle; only `NEXT_PUBLIC_` URL+anon are exposed. `.env.local` holds live keys but is
  correctly gitignored. Auth uses `getUser()` (validated, not cookie-trusted). The author page + all 7
  server actions re-check `isAdmin()` per call (`author/actions.ts:35-37`). RLS is the wall for spoiler
  tables and it's a correctly-built deny-by-default wall   reads `v_record`; no `.sql` creates it (only `v_health/v_heatmap/v_compliance_counts` exist). Every
  read hits the catch   consistency principle, not a bug - **[P1-W3] Admin nav chrome leaks onto the public archive.** The root layout wraps every route
  (including `/record`) in "Status / Author" nav; the intended pathname strip is described but not

Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â `plugin/.../AnswerNormalizer.java`),
  byte-for-byte equivalent, both carrying "change both together" warnings; the website implements no
  matching (it has no write path). Residual risk: parity is convention + selftests, not a shared CI
  test that runs both. If someone edits one regex, nothing auto-catches divergence.

### Concrete improvements
- Author the `v_record` view (the coarse, spoiler-safe projection the page already clamps through);
  build the write path as an **edge function** (never the service key in the browser). Strip nav under
  `/record`. Fix the stale copy + README + lure asset.

  script. Align the script to what actually ships tonight vs. later waves.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-07] P1-I3 companion prose/mechanics lockstep is restored.** Wren is no longer
  an authored character with no producer: right-click trust, Iss-caught reveal, one-of-three reckoning,
  and Discord-delivered reveal/reckoning lines are all wired. Rehearsal still needs to prove the pacing feels right.
- **[RESOLVED 2026-07-06] Visuals/lore lockstep for the rune font is now wired.** The keepers alphabet is
  generated, in-world rune surfaces render with `observance:runes`, and the text/resource/dashboard checks
  guard the cross-surface alphabet.
- **[F4] What DOES tie together (keep):** the oracle is one shared engine across Discord + in-world
  signs (same normalizer, same `puzzles` table, same flag gate); `hints` is a single source intended
  for both surfaces; the flag graph is internally consistent on the spine. The *machinery* coheres;
  the *surfaces and the enrichment layers* are where the orphans live.

THE FRIENDS-RUN EXPERIENCE

### The honest "is this amazing yet" answer
**Not yet, but the bones are unusually good.** A genuine, grounded, cross-surface puzzle-and-lore loop
can run once the manual launch proof is complete. The remaining gap is not missing pack audio, generic
keeper pillars, or absent rune surfaces; it is the live feel of the placed world, the still-thin per-player
illusion layer, and the companion/reckoning enrichment lane.
that would make it YouTube/ARG-critic-worthy is scaffolded, not standing.

### First 30 minutes / onboarding
- **[P1-X1] Findability still depends on real placement and a first in-world pointer.** The rosetta ring is
  no longer unbuilt in tooling, but players still need to actually find the placed route, notice the answer
  verb, and avoid accidental early ignition. Prove this in the first-hour rehearsal packet.
- **[P1-X2] "How do I answer?" is unobvious.** Editing a sign as the submission verb is a novel
  mechanic; WORLD-BUILD flags it. Without the labeled per-keeper lecterns (unwired), a group can stall
  not on the puzzle but on the *interface*.

### Pacing over weeks / async
- **[P1-X3] Dead-air becomes a cliff after Movement II.** [P1-C4]: the drip surfaces the 5 keeper
  ciphers on Discord by static rank, then the pool goes empty ### Datamining stance (veterans WILL)
- **[P2-X4] D7 "reward the datamine" is aspirational.** The design says leave a message for the xrayer
  / make the NBT item worth inspecting Â¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â none of that is built. A veteran who reads the seed sees every
  answer (RLS protects the *live* DB, not a leaked SQL file). The spoiler-safety is DB-side; the
  *design*-side "weaponize transparency" is unimplemented. Accept that a determined datamine trivializes
  the puzzle content and lean into doing/reactivity as the joy (which the D8 Golden Question already
  pushes).

### Concrete improvements
- Place/prove the rosetta ring and answer surfaces before the first session; the code can build them, but the
  launch packet must show players can discover and read them.
- Wire a minimal in-world thread pointer (a whisper or the record block) so soft-pressure has a
  producer world-side.
- Source audio + the correct pack format ## PRIORITIZED BACKLOG

| ID | P | Area | Finding | Fix sketch |
|----|---|------|---------|-----------|
| P0-D1 | Done | DB | `event_log` writer used wrong columns | fixed by `EventLogRow` mapping + plugin DB contract check |
| P0-D2 | Done | DB | `bases` upsert targeted bigint id instead of owner UUID | fixed by `owner_uuid` upsert + unique index + plugin DB contract check |
| P0-V1 | Done | Structures | Rich per-keeper templates + rosetta were not placed | fixed with `StructureTemplates.keeper(...)` placement paths + world-build guard |
| P0-A1 | Done | Plugin | 5 signature beats were coded but unregistered | fixed in `BeatLibrary.registerDefaults()` + plugin compile guard |
| P0-R1 | Done | Pack | `pack_format` drifted from server 1.21.11 | fixed with `[75,0]` resourcepack metadata + asset guard |
| P0-R2 | Done | Pack | Zero `.ogg` files made all sound/mood silent | fixed with 11 checked mono OGG files + media guard |
| P0-C1 | Done | Content | Companion/reckoning/co-op producers were believed absent | fixed with Wren/coop/Seventh producers + Discord consumers + contract guard |
| P0-C6 | P0 | Content | Seed re-run order footgun: dark rows OR leaked M4 answers | add db:seed runner enforcing 0006-first order |
| P0-D3 | Done | DB | `world_paste_ledger` support was missing from the DB bundle | fixed by `0012_world_paste_ledger.sql` + `schema-repair.sql` |
| P1-A2 | Done | Plugin | Beat double-fire on restart / 2 instances (no atomic claim) | fixed with `approved -> firing` DB claim |
| P1-A6 | P1 | Plugin | `PerPlayer` missing showEntity/packet-light/fog | build the 3 primitives (pre-Phase-3) |
| P1-A7 | Done | Plugin | Rune font had no in-world surface use | fixed with rune crib/beat font use + text/resource/dashboard guards |
| P1-D4 | Done | DB | Remote `watcher_sleep` could not parse jsonb | fixed by `SettingsRow.value` as `JsonElement` + plugin DB contract check |
| P1-D5 | P1 | DB | Dossiers/compliance render blank in dashboard (read drift) | view/mapping to reconcile read columns |
